# Grothendieck's unproved claims and conjectures in the Pro notes: a map (17 Nov 2026)

Built by `grothendieck_archive_transcription_20261110/tools_20261113/claims_map.py` (script, no model) from the research notes of the returned Pro transcription packages (GPT-6 Astra, ChatGPT Pro mode). An entry is listed when the note's kind says "unproved claim" or "conjecture". Nothing here is a verdict: the notes are first-pass leads, and an entry that names one of our runs may or may not be settled by it.

- Entries: 1387 in 52 packages.
- Naming one of our runs or prompts: 250; these are tied to our work and reach the runs' readers through the cross-links.
- Naming none: 1137. No note ties them to our work yet. They are listed below by the package's group in the plan, for the companions' designers and the prompt writers to read first.

Full table (every entry, with the runs it names): `GROTHENDIECK_CLAIMS_MAP_20261117.tsv`. The full text of an entry is in `grothendieck_archive_transcription_20261110/returns/<package>_unzipped/<package>_notes.md`.
